# does not pass through: prepending it CONTAMINATES an otherwise-clean within-restored stream to X7 -- the
# gate refuses to certify a history carrying an unlicensed beyond-ceiling held-authority record. (3) Only a
# seed WITHIN the ceiling is inert (the clean stream stays clean). (4) A lone seed (no re-widen event) is
# clean either way -- a single snapshot is not a re-widen. Empirically validated on real gate bytes.
try:
    _seed_wide = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": 0, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"], "target_scopes": ["*"]}}
    _seed_within = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": 0, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}
    _beyond = _honest_stream(_R, effects=("READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"))
    _within = _honest_stream(_R)
    seed_beyond_x7 = _viol(G.check_authority_history([_seed_wide] + _beyond, grants=_R["grants"]))    # beyond-ceiling seed = grant-independent target-widening -> X7 survives
    noseed_beyond_x7 = _uncert(G.check_authority_history(_beyond, grants=_R["grants"]))
    within_clean = _uncert(G.check_authority_history(_within, grants=_R["grants"]))
    wide_seed_contaminates = _viol(G.check_authority_history([_seed_wide] + _within, grants=_R["grants"]))  # seed contaminates a clean stream to X7 (grant-independent)
    within_seed_inert = _uncert(G.check_authority_history([_seed_within] + _within, grants=_R["grants"]))
    lone_wide_seed_clean = G.check_authority_history([_seed_wide], grants=_R["grants"]) == []
    record("CE161", "PROBE-2 (V29 withdrawal): an armed beyond-ceiling re-widen is X7U offline, but a forged beyond-ceiling wide SEED is a grant-independent target-widening -> X7 (it cannot ride along: it contaminates the stream to X7); a within-ceiling seed is inert (armed re-widen stays X7U); a lone seed (no re-widen) is clean", "B-PROBE2-P30",
           seed_beyond_x7 and noseed_beyond_x7 and within_clean and wide_seed_contaminates and within_seed_inert and lone_wide_seed_clean,
           f"seed_beyond_x7={seed_beyond_x7} noseed_beyond_x7={noseed_beyond_x7} within_clean={within_clean} wide_seed_contaminates={wide_seed_contaminates} within_seed_inert={within_seed_inert} lone_wide_seed_clean={lone_wide_seed_clean}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE161", "PROBE-2 forged-seed cannot inflate or ride along", "B-PROBE2-P30", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE162 / Kimi-A3 (V28 regression): the gate's OWN honest degraded-first LIFT->recover must NOT be flagged as
# X7 (V28 caught B's predecessor over-flagging it). The REAL gate record stream, anchored to the runtime's
# own grant, audits CLEAN (no false positive on the gate's authenticated recovery).
try:
    _honest_recovery_clean = _uncert(G.check_authority_history(_R["records"], grants=_R["grants"]))
    _unanchored_x7 = _uncert(G.check_authority_history(_R["records"]))  # V29 withdrawal: armed re-widen -> X7U
    record("CE162", "Kimi-A3: the gate's OWN honest degraded-first recovery is NOT a violation -- it is X7U (uncertifiable, not a false positive), identical with or without the grant", "B-A3-P30",
           _honest_recovery_clean and _unanchored_x7, f"recovery_clean_anchored={_honest_recovery_clean} unanchored_x7={_unanchored_x7}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE162", "Kimi-A3 honest recovery not over-flagged", "B-A3-P30", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE163 / B DISCLOSED RESIDUAL (NEGATIVE CE -- boundary, not a silent pass): B relocates trust from the
# unauthenticated stream marker to the grant-export's PROVENANCE. The content-blind offline checker cannot
# distinguish an AUTHENTIC grant export from a FORGED one, so a caller who fabricates the grants dict spoofs
# the check. Asserted as a BOUNDARY: an authentic grant (restored {READ,WRITE}) catches an over-wide DELETE
# re-widen as X7 (divergence); a FABRICATED grant claiming restored {READ,WRITE,DELETE} passes the SAME
# re-widen clean. The forged-grant spoof is OUT of the offline threat model. This is the sole place a grants
# literal appears -- to DEMONSTRATE the residual, never to certify a recovery (that would be the vacuity).
try:
    _beyond = _honest_stream(_R, effects=("READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"))
    _auth = G.check_authority_history(_beyond, grants=_R["grants"])
    _forged_grant = {_R["session"]: {"correction_digest": _R["marker"]["correction_digest"], "landing_epoch": _R["landing"],
                                     "restored_effects": frozenset({"READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"}), "restored_targets": _R["restored_targets"]}}
    _forg = G.check_authority_history(_beyond, grants=_forged_grant)
    _residual_closed = _uncert(_auth) and _uncert(_forg) and _auth == _forg
    record("CE163", "RESIDUAL CLOSED (V29 withdrawal): an authentic and a fabricated grant now yield the IDENTICAL X7U -- there is no positive certification left to spoof; the forged-grant residual is eliminated at the root", "B-RESIDUAL-P30",
           _residual_closed, f"authentic={_auth} forged={_forg} identical_uncertifiable={_residual_closed}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE163", "B disclosed forged-grant residual", "B-RESIDUAL-P30", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE164 / GPT-P30-01 (V29 EFFECT BYPASS -- CLOSED): a widening REJECTED before a genuine terminal-lift cannot
# launder through the lift. The rejected DELETE recovery is DENY_FALSE at the runtime (the re-establish branch
# is bounded by the de-poisoned contract_ceiling), the ceiling and grant restore only demonstrably-held
# authority (no DELETE), and the checker stays honest. This was the FIRST reproduced real effect bypass in many
# rounds; it is locked here as a CURRENT-BYTE regression guard for CLAIM-X7-BREADTH / CLAIM-GRANT-ANCHOR.
try:
    def _amd164(cid="cor-poison"):
        _adj = lambda i, d: {"adjudicator_id": i, "control_domain": d, "signature": T.signature("valid", kid=i)}
        return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": cid,
                "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"prior"),
                "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED", "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"},
                "session_id": "sess-0001", "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
                "adjudicators": [_adj("adj-1", "audit-domain"), _adj("adj-2", "ops-domain")],
                "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z",
                "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
    _f = T.GateFixture()  # contract A = READ,WRITE
    _body = T.contract_body(_f.policy_digest)
    _body["allowed_effects"] = ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"]
    _body["effect_registry"]["DELETE"] = {"adapter_digest": T.ADAPTER_DIGEST}
    _body["degraded_effects"] = ["READ"]
    _c2 = G.digest(_body); _f.gate.contracts[_c2] = {"body": copy.deepcopy(_body), "revoked": False}
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="a" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=7, sequence=11), epoch=5))
    _rB = T.effect_request(_c2, _f.policy_digest, effect="DELETE", nonce="b" * 32)
    _f.gate.authorize(_rB, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=8, sequence=12), epoch=6))  # rejected widening
    _cc = set(_f.gate.state.contract_ceiling["sess-0001"].effects)
    _f.gate.submit_amendment(_amd164())
    _gr = G.grants_from_state(_f.gate)
    _rB2 = T.effect_request(_c2, _f.policy_digest, effect="DELETE", nonce="c" * 32)
    _d3 = _f.gate.authorize(_rB2, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=9, sequence=13), epoch=7))
    _rewiden_denied = _d3.get("decision") == G.DECISION_DENY_FALSE and "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING" in (_d3.get("reason_codes") or [])
    _ceiling_clean = "DELETE" not in _cc
    _grant_clean = all("DELETE" not in set(g.get("restored_effects") or ()) for g in _gr.get("sess-0001", []))
    _checker_honest = G.check_authority_history(_f.gate.state.records, grants=_gr) == []
    record("CE164", "GPT-P30-01 EFFECT BYPASS CLOSED: a widening rejected before a genuine lift cannot launder through it (runtime DENY_FALSE; ceiling+grant carry no DELETE; checker honest)", "GPT-P30-01",
           _rewiden_denied and _ceiling_clean and _grant_clean and _checker_honest,
           f"rewiden_denied={_rewiden_denied} ceiling_no_delete={_ceiling_clean} grant_no_delete={_grant_clean} checker_honest={_checker_honest}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE164", "GPT-P30-01 effect bypass closed", "GPT-P30-01", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE165 / GPT-P30-02 (multi-cycle checker soundness): a CONFORMANT two-amendment history audits CLEAN -- each
# re-widen validated against ITS landing amendment's grant via the per-session grant LEDGER (list). Overwriting
# to keep only the latest grant made a legitimate second-amendment history falsely audit ['X7','X7'].
try:
    def _amd165(cid):
        _adj = lambda i, d: {"adjudicator_id": i, "control_domain": d, "signature": T.signature("valid", kid=i)}
        return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": cid,
                "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(("prior-" + cid).encode()),
                "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED", "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"},
                "session_id": "sess-0001", "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
                "adjudicators": [_adj("adj-1", "audit-domain"), _adj("adj-2", "ops-domain")],
                "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z",
                "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
    _f = T.GateFixture(degraded_effects=["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"], degraded_targets=["https://db.example.internal/"])
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="a" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=7, sequence=11), epoch=5))
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="b" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=8, sequence=12), epoch=6))
    _f.gate.submit_amendment(_amd165("cor-one"))
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="c" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=9, sequence=13), epoch=7))
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="d" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=10, sequence=14), epoch=8))
    _f.gate.submit_amendment(_amd165("cor-two"))
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="e" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=11, sequence=15), epoch=9))
    _grants = G.grants_from_state(_f.gate)
    _two_clean = _uncert(G.check_authority_history(_f.gate.state.records, grants=_grants))
    _ledger_two = len(_grants.get("sess-0001", [])) == 2
    record("CE165", "GPT-P30-02 multi-cycle: a conformant two-amendment history is X7U offline (each cycle uncertifiable, grant inert); the per-session ledger remains a runtime diagnostic", "GPT-P30-02",
           _two_clean and _ledger_two, f"two_amendment_clean={_two_clean} ledger_size={len(_grants.get('sess-0001', []))}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE165", "GPT-P30-02 multi-cycle checker soundness", "GPT-P30-02", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE166 / GPT-P30-03 + Kimi K30-A4 (abort soundness BOTH directions): the abort must RECOVER a determinate
# clock- and verifier-independent witness FALSE when the injected verifier raises (subject mismatch -> DENY_FALSE,
# not masked to UNKNOWN); and must NOT FABRICATE one clock-free (health=FAILED under a NON-inverted future lease
# with a first-clock fault -> DENY_UNKNOWN, never a FALSE manufactured from an attacker-influenceable observed_at).
try:
    _fa = T.GateFixture()
    def _vr(kind, payload, sig):
        if kind == "witness":
            raise RuntimeError("witness verifier unavailable")
        return True
    _fa.gate.verify_signature = _vr
    _da = _fa.gate.authorize(_fa.request(nonce="9" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(subject="other-agent")))
    _mask_recovered = _da.get("decision") == G.DECISION_DENY_FALSE   # determinate FALSE recovered, not masked
    _fb = T.GateFixture()
    _fb.gate.authorize(_fb.request(nonce="1" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _fb.gate.clock = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(RuntimeError("first-clock fault"))
    _rc = T.witness_receipt(health="FAILED"); _rc["observed_at"] = "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"; _rc["expires_at"] = "2099-12-31T00:00:00Z"
    _db = _fb.gate.authorize(_fb.request(nonce="2" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=_rc))
    _no_fabrication = _db.get("decision") == G.DECISION_DENY_UNKNOWN   # health=FAILED not credited clock-free
    record("CE166", "GPT-P30-03 + K30-A4 abort soundness both ways: recovers a determinate witness FALSE on a raising verifier (not masked); never fabricates one clock-free (not manufactured)", "GPT-P30-03/K30-A4",
           _mask_recovered and _no_fabrication, f"mask_recovered_false={_mask_recovered} no_clockfree_fabrication={_no_fabrication}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE166", "GPT-P30-03 + K30-A4 abort soundness", "GPT-P30-03/K30-A4", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE167 / GPT-P30-05 (MAJOR liveness/totality): a dependency-originated NON-Exception BaseException raised by
# an injected clock/verifier must FAIL CLOSED at every public boundary -- authorized_issuance_bound returns
# False; commit returns a recorded FAILED_NO_EFFECT and leaves NO token stuck in COMMITTING (VOID); amendment
# records a correction-outcome and does not escape. KeyboardInterrupt / SystemExit MUST still propagate, and
# even then the COMMITTING reservation is rolled back. Reproduced verbatim from GPT's independent PROBE-3/4/5.
try:
    class _EvilBase(BaseException): pass
    def _amd():
        return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": "cor-base",
                "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"p"), "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED",
                "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"}, "session_id": "sess-0001", "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
                "adjudicators": [{"adjudicator_id": "adj-1", "control_domain": "audit-domain", "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="adj-1")},
                                 {"adjudicator_id": "adj-2", "control_domain": "ops-domain", "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="adj-2")}],
                "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z", "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
    # AIB
    _fa = T.GateFixture(); _da = _fa.gate.authorize(_fa.request(nonce="2" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _fa.gate.clock = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(_EvilBase("aib"))
    try:
        _aib_closed = _fa.gate.authorized_issuance_bound(_da) is False
    except BaseException:
        _aib_closed = False
    # commit -> no COMMITTING strand, recorded failure, no escape
    _fc = T.GateFixture(); _dc = _fc.gate.authorize(_fc.request(nonce="3" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _rc = _dc["authorization_receipt"]; _tok = _rc["commit_token"]["token_id"]
    _bind = {k: _rc[k] for k in ("request_digest", "canonical_target", "params_digest", "adapter_digest", "effect_type")}
    _fc.gate.clock = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(_EvilBase("commit"))
    try:
        _co = _fc.gate.commit(_tok, _bind, T.evidence_bundle())
        _commit_closed = (_co.get("outcome") == "FAILED_NO_EFFECT" and "E-COMMIT-ABORTED" in (_co.get("reason_codes") or [])
                          and _fc.gate.state.tokens[_tok]["status"] == "VOID")
    except BaseException:
        _commit_closed = False
    # amendment -> recorded, not escaped
    _fm = T.GateFixture(degraded_effects=["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"], degraded_targets=["https://db.example.internal/"])
    _fm.gate.authorize(_fm.request(nonce="4" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _fm.gate.authorize(_fm.request(nonce="5" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=8)))
    _pre = len(_fm.gate.state.records); _fm.gate.verify_signature = lambda *a, **k: (_ for _ in ()).throw(_EvilBase("amend"))
    try:
        _mo = _fm.gate.submit_amendment(_amd())
        _amend_closed = (_mo.get("accepted") is not True and len(_fm.gate.state.records) - _pre >= 1)
    except BaseException:
        _amend_closed = False
    # KeyboardInterrupt still propagates, and the COMMITTING reservation is rolled back to VOID
    _fk = T.GateFixture(); _dk = _fk.gate.authorize(_fk.request(nonce="6" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _rk = _dk["authorization_receipt"]; _tk = _rk["commit_token"]["token_id"]
    _bk = {k: _rk[k] for k in ("request_digest", "canonical_target", "params_digest", "adapter_digest", "effect_type")}
    _fk.gate.clock = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(KeyboardInterrupt())
    try:
        _fk.gate.commit(_tk, _bk, T.evidence_bundle()); _ki_prop = False
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        _ki_prop = (_fk.gate.state.tokens[_tk]["status"] == "VOID")
    except BaseException:
        _ki_prop = False
    record("CE167", "GPT-P30-05 BaseException totality/liveness: a dependency-originated non-Exception BaseException fails closed at AIB/commit/amendment (no COMMITTING strand, outcome recorded, no escape); KeyboardInterrupt/SystemExit still propagate with rollback", "GPT-P30-05",
           _aib_closed and _commit_closed and _amend_closed and _ki_prop,
           f"aib_closed={_aib_closed} commit_closed_no_strand={_commit_closed} amend_recorded={_amend_closed} ki_propagates_voided={_ki_prop}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE167", "GPT-P30-05 BaseException totality/liveness", "GPT-P30-05", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE168 / GPT-P30-04 (MAJOR precision): authorized_issuance_bound reads the clock INSIDE the commit lock, at
# the SAME linearization point as the entry snapshot -- so the unexpired check is evaluated at the instant of
# the locked snapshot and a token that expires while the predicate waits for the lock is observed expired.
# Deterministic single-threaded probe: instrument the clock reader to record whether the commit lock is HELD
# at the instant the clock is read; under the fix it MUST be held (pre-fix it was read before the lock).
try:
    _f = T.GateFixture(); _d = _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="2" * 32), T.evidence_bundle())
    _held = {"v": None}
    _orig_clock = _f.gate._read_clock
    def _probe_clock():
        _held["v"] = _f.gate._commit_lock.locked()   # threading.Lock.locked() -> True iff held
        return _orig_clock()
    _f.gate._read_clock = _probe_clock
    _r = _f.gate.authorized_issuance_bound(_d)
    _clock_in_lock = _held["v"] is True
    record("CE168", "GPT-P30-04: authorized_issuance_bound reads the clock INSIDE the commit lock (precise point-in-time linearization -- a token expiring while the predicate waits for the lock is observed expired, not certified on stale pre-lock time)", "GPT-P30-04",
           _clock_in_lock and _r is True, f"clock_read_under_lock={_clock_in_lock} baseline_true={_r}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE168", "GPT-P30-04 in-lock clock read", "GPT-P30-04", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")


green = sum(1 for *_, ok, _ in results if ok)

# ============================================================================
# V30 REGRESSION CEs (GLM-F1/K32-01, GPT-P32-01, K32-03, K32-04)
# ============================================================================

# CE169 / GLM-F1 + K32-01 (cross-contract ceiling-poison -- CLOSED): exercises the ACTUAL DEGRADED vector.
# V31 GLM-F1 correction: the prior CE169 used a HEALTHY witness which terminalizes via E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING
# before the fix path executes, making CE169 non-load-bearing (it passed GREEN even with the fix reverted).
# This rewrite uses a DEGRADED C2 DELETE request (the actual CLASS A vector): the degraded ceiling {READ}
# is a subset of {READ,WRITE} (narrowing passes), the pending flag IS set, but the fix prevents the
# DENIED request from writing contract_ceiling. After lift, DELETE is correctly DENIED.
try:
    def _reg_c2_169(fixture):
        body = T.contract_body(fixture.policy_digest)
        body["allowed_effects"] = ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"]
        body["effect_registry"]["DELETE"] = {"adapter_digest": T.ADAPTER_DIGEST}
        body["degraded_effects"] = ["READ"]
        d = G.digest(body); fixture.gate.contracts[d] = {"body": copy.deepcopy(body), "revoked": False}
        return d
    def _amd169(session="sess-0001", cid="cor-169"):
        _adj = lambda i, d: {"adjudicator_id": i, "control_domain": d, "signature": T.signature("valid", kid=i)}
        return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": cid,
                "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"prior"),
                "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED", "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"},
                "session_id": session, "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
                "adjudicators": [_adj("adj-1", "audit-domain"), _adj("adj-2", "ops-domain")],
                "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z",
                "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
    _f = T.GateFixture()
    _c2 = _reg_c2_169(_f)
    # establish under C1 HEALTHY
    _f.gate.authorize(_f.request(nonce="a" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=7, sequence=11), epoch=5))
    # DENIED degraded C2 DELETE -- the actual CLASS A vector (DEGRADED witness: narrowing passes, effect denied)
    _rB = T.effect_request(_c2, _f.policy_digest, effect="DELETE", nonce="b" * 32)
    _f.gate.authorize(_rB, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=8, sequence=12), epoch=6))
    _cc_clean = "DELETE" not in set(_f.gate.state.contract_ceiling.get("sess-0001", G.Authority([], [])).effects)
    # terminalize + lift
    _f.gate.submit_amendment(_amd169())
    # retry DELETE under C2 -- must be DENIED
    _rB2 = T.effect_request(_c2, _f.policy_digest, effect="DELETE", nonce="c" * 32)
    _d3 = _f.gate.authorize(_rB2, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=9, sequence=13), epoch=7))
    _exploit_denied = _d3.get("decision") == G.DECISION_DENY_FALSE
    record("CE169", "GLM-F1/K32-01 DEGRADED-vector ceiling-poison CLOSED: a DENIED degraded C2 DELETE does not poison contract_ceiling; post-lift re-establish for DELETE is DENY_FALSE", "GLM-F1",
           _cc_clean and _exploit_denied, f"ceiling_no_delete={_cc_clean} exploit_denied={_exploit_denied}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE169", "GLM-F1 cross-contract ceiling poison", "GLM-F1", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE173 / K33-01 (ALLOWed-degraded ceiling-poison -- the 32nd instance -- CLOSED): an ALLOWed degraded C2
# READ must NOT poison contract_ceiling with C2's full HEALTHY ceiling. The V30 fix closed DENIED-request
# poison; K33-01 is ALLOWED-request poison. The V31 fix writes contract_ceiling ONLY on HEALTHY ALLOWs.
try:
    _f173 = T.GateFixture()
    _c2_173 = _reg_c2_169(_f173)
    # establish under C1 HEALTHY
    _f173.gate.authorize(_f173.request(nonce="a" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=7, sequence=11), epoch=5))
    _cc_pre = set(_f173.gate.state.contract_ceiling.get("sess-0001", G.Authority([], [])).effects)
    # ALLOWed degraded C2 READ -- the K33-01 vector (READ is in C2's degraded ceiling)
    _rD = T.effect_request(_c2_173, _f173.policy_digest, effect="READ", nonce="d" * 32)
    _dD = _f173.gate.authorize(_rD, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=8, sequence=12), epoch=6))
    _allowed = _dD.get("decision") == "ALLOW"
    _cc_post = set(_f173.gate.state.contract_ceiling.get("sess-0001", G.Authority([], [])).effects)
    _no_delete_leak = "DELETE" not in _cc_post
    _cc_unchanged = _cc_post == _cc_pre  # degraded ALLOW must not change contract_ceiling
    # terminalize + lift + retry DELETE
    _f173.gate.authorize(_f173.request(effect="READ", nonce="e" * 32), T.evidence_bundle(
        receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="HEALTHY", epoch=9, sequence=13), epoch=7))
    _f173.gate.submit_amendment(_amd169(cid="cor-173"))
    _rDel = T.effect_request(_c2_173, _f173.policy_digest, effect="DELETE", nonce="f" * 32)
    _dDel = _f173.gate.authorize(_rDel, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="HEALTHY", epoch=10, sequence=14), epoch=8))
    _delete_denied = _dDel.get("decision") != "ALLOW"
    record("CE173", "K33-01 ALLOWed-degraded ceiling-poison CLOSED: an ALLOWed degraded C2 READ does not broaden contract_ceiling; post-lift DELETE is DENIED", "K33-01",
           _allowed and _no_delete_leak and _cc_unchanged and _delete_denied,
           f"allowed={_allowed} no_delete_leak={_no_delete_leak} cc_unchanged={_cc_unchanged} delete_denied={_delete_denied}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE173", "K33-01 ALLOWed-degraded ceiling poison", "K33-01", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE170 / GPT-P32-01 (same-epoch over-withdrawal -- CLOSED): an armed same-epoch ceiling CHANGE must be X7
# (grant-independent), not X7U. An armed same-epoch EQUAL snapshot is X7U (the re-widen being tracked).
# Constructed as: base(ep=1) -> event -> marker(ep=2) -> first_at_3(armed re-widen, X7U, consumed) -> second_at_3.
# The second_at_3 record is at the same epoch as first_at_3 with the marker already consumed.
# We use a separate two-record-after-marker construction with epoch_floor avoidance.
try:
    _ev170 = _R["event"]
    _mk170 = _R["marker"]
    S170 = _R["session"]
    _base170 = {"session_id": S170, "authority_epoch": 1, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}
    # The marker's authority_epoch is 2, licensed_epoch is 3. After the marker, prev from base is (1, {READ}).
    # A record at ep=3 (the licensed_epoch, strictly forward from prev_ep=1) would be the armed re-widen -> X7U.
    # Then a SECOND record at ep=3 is same-epoch with prev_ep=3 (marker consumed, no longer armed).
    # For the changed test: use a single stream with the changed record at same-epoch as a prior record.
    # Simplest correct test: base(ep=1) -> [no marker] -> same-epoch-changed(ep=1). This is the unarmed case.
    # For armed: use GPT's reproducer directly (which proved the fix on canonical bytes).
    _unarmed_stream = [_base170, {"session_id": S170, "authority_epoch": 1,
                       "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}]
    _unarmed_changed_x7 = "X7" in G.check_authority_history(_unarmed_stream)
    # Armed test via GPT's reproducer pattern: event + marker + same-epoch-changed + same-epoch-equal
    # using the reproducer's exact pattern (which was confirmed X7 on fixed bytes above).
    import test_bssg_gate as T
    _ev_170b = {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": "cor-170",
                "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"p"), "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED",
                "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"}, "session_id": "s170", "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
                "adjudicators": [{"adjudicator_id": "adj-1", "control_domain": "audit-domain", "signature": T.signature("valid", "adj-1")},
                                  {"adjudicator_id": "adj-2", "control_domain": "legal-domain", "signature": T.signature("valid", "adj-2")}],
                "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z", "signature": T.signature("valid", "ops-key")}
    _mk_170b = {"record_type": "bssg.correction-accepted", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "gate_version": G.GATE_VERSION,
                "correction_id": "cor-170", "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "session_id": "s170",
                "authority_epoch": 2, "licensed_epoch": 3, "correction_digest": G.digest(_ev_170b), "adjudicators_verified": 2}
    _b170 = {"session_id": "s170", "authority_epoch": 2, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ"], "target_scopes": ["a"]}}
    _sc170 = {"session_id": "s170", "authority_epoch": 2, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE"], "target_scopes": ["a"]}}
    _se170 = {"session_id": "s170", "authority_epoch": 2, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ"], "target_scopes": ["a"]}}
    _armed_changed_x7 = "X7" in G.check_authority_history([_b170, _ev_170b, _mk_170b, _sc170])
    _armed_equal_x7u = _uncert(G.check_authority_history([_b170, _ev_170b, _mk_170b, _se170]))
    record("CE170", "GPT-P32-01 same-epoch over-withdrawal CLOSED: unarmed same-epoch ceiling CHANGE is X7; armed same-epoch ceiling CHANGE is X7 (grant-independent); armed same-epoch EQUAL is X7U", "GPT-P32-01",
           _unarmed_changed_x7 and _armed_changed_x7 and _armed_equal_x7u,
           f"unarmed_changed_x7={_unarmed_changed_x7} armed_changed_x7={_armed_changed_x7} armed_equal_x7u={_armed_equal_x7u}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE170", "GPT-P32-01 same-epoch over-withdrawal", "GPT-P32-01", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE171 / K32-03 (malformed authority_snapshot -- CLOSED): a PRESENT but non-dict authority_snapshot
# (string, int) in an armed position is grant-independent type-tamper -> X7. An ABSENT snapshot (None)
# means no authority to analyze and is skipped (no violation).
try:
    _ev171 = _R["event"]
    _mk171 = _R["marker"]
    _base171 = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": 1, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}
    _str_snap = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": _R["landing"], "authority_snapshot": "tampered"}
    _int_snap = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": _R["landing"], "authority_snapshot": 42}
    _absent = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": _R["landing"]}
    _str_x7 = "X7" in G.check_authority_history([_base171, _ev171, _mk171, _str_snap])
    _int_x7 = "X7" in G.check_authority_history([_base171, _ev171, _mk171, _int_snap])
    _absent_clean = G.check_authority_history([_base171, _ev171, _mk171, _absent]) == []
    record("CE171", "K32-03 malformed snapshot CLOSED: armed + string/int snapshot is X7 (type-tamper); armed + absent snapshot is skipped (clean)", "K32-03",
           _str_x7 and _int_x7 and _absent_clean, f"str_x7={_str_x7} int_x7={_int_x7} absent_clean={_absent_clean}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE171", "K32-03 malformed snapshot", "K32-03", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# CE172 / K32-04 (marker metadata gaps -- CLOSED): a marker with missing licensed_epoch fails
# _is_acceptance_marker and does not arm the X7U branch; the widening is unlicensed -> X7.
try:
    _ev172 = _R["event"]
    _mk_good = _R["marker"]
    _mk_no_le = {k: v for k, v in _mk_good.items() if k != "licensed_epoch"}
    _base172 = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": 1, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}
    _wide172 = {"session_id": _R["session"], "authority_epoch": _R["landing"],
                "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE"], "target_scopes": [_REAL_TGT]}}
    _good_x7u = _uncert(G.check_authority_history([_base172, _ev172, _mk_good, _wide172]))
    _no_le_x7 = "X7" in G.check_authority_history([_base172, _ev172, _mk_no_le, _wide172])
    record("CE172", "K32-04 marker metadata CLOSED: well-formed marker arms X7U; missing licensed_epoch fails marker validation -> widening is unlicensed X7", "K32-04",
           _good_x7u and _no_le_x7, f"good_x7u={_good_x7u} no_le_x7={_no_le_x7}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE172", "K32-04 marker metadata gaps", "K32-04", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")


green = sum(1 for *_, ok, _ in results if ok)
# (intermediate summary suppressed -- final summary is at the end of the file after all CEs)

# CE174 / AUDIT-V33-01 (commit pre-reserve BaseException totality -- same class as GPT-P30-05/CE167):
# a hostile dict subclass whose .items() raises BaseException must fail closed at the commit ingress
# boundary (VOID + failure record), not escape the public API leaving the token UNUSED with no record.
try:
    class _HostileBE174(dict):
        def items(self):
            raise BaseException("hostile-items-174")
    _f174 = T.GateFixture()
    _d174 = _f174.gate.authorize(_f174.request(nonce="ce174" + "0" * 27), T.evidence_bundle())
    _rcpt174 = _d174["authorization_receipt"]
    _tid174 = _rcpt174["commit_token"]["token_id"]
    _binding174 = _HostileBE174({
        "request_digest": _rcpt174["request_digest"], "canonical_target": _rcpt174["canonical_target"],
        "params_digest": _rcpt174["params_digest"], "adapter_digest": _rcpt174["adapter_digest"],
        "effect_type": _rcpt174["effect_type"],
    })
    _escaped = False
    try:
        _r174 = _f174.gate.commit(_tid174, _binding174, T.evidence_bundle())
        _closed = _r174.get("outcome") == "FAILED_NO_EFFECT"
    except BaseException:
        _escaped = True
        _closed = False
    _status174 = _f174.gate.state.tokens.get(_tid174, {}).get("status", "UNKNOWN")
    record("CE174", "AUDIT-V33-01 commit pre-reserve BaseException totality: a hostile BaseException-raising binding fails closed (VOID + failure record), does not escape the public API", "AUDIT-V33-01",
           not _escaped and _closed and _status174 == "VOID",
           f"escaped={_escaped} closed={_closed} token_status={_status174}")
except Exception as e:
    record("CE174", "AUDIT-V33-01 commit BaseException totality", "AUDIT-V33-01", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

# Final summary (after all CEs including V33 additions)
green = sum(1 for *_, ok, _ in results if ok)
print(f"\n{green}/{len(results)} GREEN  ({len(results)-green} RED = open defects)")


======== bssg_model_check.py ========
"""BSSG -- OPERATIONAL model-checker (R7 rebuild).

The prior model-checker proved properties of a hand-copied transition graph; GPT's audit
showed the executable violated one of them (allow-after-deny), so an abstract-graph proof
does not bind the implementation. This version establishes a REFINEMENT RELATION: it drives
the REAL gate through positive and adversarial traces and checks that (a) every observed
operation lands in a state the Section-14 model permits, and (b) the model's safety
invariants hold on actual executions. It also keeps the two sound abstract checks (kernel
truth table, antitone degradation) and removes the dead strictness code from finding KIMI-1
(strictness is a gate behavior, checked operationally in OP-STRICT).

On the un-repaired gate the operational checks FAIL (they are the audit counterexamples as
model invariants); on the repaired gate they PASS. Run: python3 bssg_model_check.py
"""
import os, sys, copy
from itertools import product, chain, combinations

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import bssg_gate as G
import test_bssg_gate as T

fail = []
def check(name, ok, detail=""):
    print(("PASS " if ok else "FAIL ") + name + (("  " + detail) if detail else ""))
    if not ok:
        fail.append(name)

def subsets(s):
    s = list(s)
    return [frozenset(c) for c in chain.from_iterable(combinations(s, r) for r in range(len(s)+1))]

# ===========================================================================
# ABSTRACT CHECK 1 -- kernel truth table, re-derived from the gate's own code.
# ===========================================================================
TV = G.TRUTH_VALUES; conj = list(G.KERNEL_CONJUNCTS); n = 0; ok = True
for combo in product(TV, repeat=5):
    dec, _ = G.kernel_decision(dict(zip(conj, combo)))
    any_false = any(v == G.FALSE for v in combo); any_unknown = any(v == G.UNKNOWN for v in combo)
    exp = G.DECISION_DENY_FALSE if any_false else (G.DECISION_DENY_UNKNOWN if any_unknown else G.DECISION_ALLOW)
    if dec != exp or ((dec == G.DECISION_ALLOW) != all(v == G.TRUE for v in combo)):
        ok = False; break
    n += 1
check("A1 kernel truth table: gate matches spec rule on all 3^5 states + ALLOW iff all TRUE", ok and n == 243, "states=%d" % n)

# ===========================================================================
# ABSTRACT CHECK 2 -- degradation antitone (subset) over the finite lattice.
# (KIMI-1 fix: the dead 'strict_ok' branch is gone; strictness is checked in OP-STRICT.)
# ===========================================================================
U_eff = ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"]; U_tgt = ["a", "b", "c"]; healths = ["HEALTHY", "DEGRADED", "FAILED", "UNKNOWN"]
anti = True; contracts = 0
for he in subsets(U_eff):
    for ht in subsets(U_tgt):
        for de in subsets(he):
            for dt in subsets(ht):
                c = {"allowed_effects": list(he), "allowed_target_scopes": list(ht),
                     "degraded_effects": list(de), "degraded_target_scopes": list(dt)}
                ceil = {h: G.ceiling_for_health(c, h) for h in healths}; contracts += 1
                for h1 in healths:
                    for h2 in healths:
                        if G.HEALTH_ORDER[h1] <= G.HEALTH_ORDER[h2] and not (ceil[h1] <= ceil[h2]):
                            anti = False
check("A2 degradation antitone over the full finite lattice (health down => ceiling subset)", anti, "contracts=%d" % contracts)

# ===========================================================================
# OPERATIONAL REFINEMENT -- drive the REAL gate; observed transitions must be
# permitted by Section 14, and the safety invariants must hold on real traces.
# ===========================================================================
PERMITTED = {
    "RECEIVED": {"REJECTED_SCHEMA", "EVALUATING"},
    "EVALUATING": {"DENIED_FALSE", "DENIED_UNKNOWN", "AUTHORIZED_UNUSED", "SESSION_TERMINAL"},
    "AUTHORIZED_UNUSED": {"COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME", "FAILED_NO_EFFECT"},
    "SESSION_TERMINAL": {"EVALUATING"},
}
TERMINAL_REASONS = {"E-SESSION-TERMINAL", "E-REQUEST-DENIED-TERMINAL", "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING", "E-NONSTRICT-NARROWING"}
def st_auth(d):
    r = d.get("reason_codes", [])
    # F (F-BSSG-P17-GPT-05 / KIMI-6): the model projection uses the SAME positive-issuance predicate as
    # the product path, so a collision / issuance-failure record (decision=ALLOW, issuance_status=
    # FAILED_NO_EFFECT) is NOT modeled as authorized.
    if G.BSSGGate.authorized_issuance(d): return "AUTHORIZED_UNUSED"
    if d.get("issuance_status") == "FAILED_NO_EFFECT": return "DENIED_UNKNOWN"  # authorizable but no effect
    if any(x in TERMINAL_REASONS for x in r): return "SESSION_TERMINAL"
    if d["decision"] == "DENY_FALSE": return "DENIED_FALSE"
    if d["decision"] == "DENY_UNKNOWN": return "DENIED_UNKNOWN"
    if str(d["decision"]).startswith("CRASH"): return "CRASH"
    return "REJECTED_SCHEMA"
def st_commit(c):
    return "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME" if c["outcome"] == "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME" else "FAILED_NO_EFFECT"

observed = []  # (from_state, to_state) collected across every scenario
def auth(f, r, ev):
    try:
        d = f.gate.authorize(r, ev)
    except Exception as e:  # totality: a decision function must never raise
        d = {"decision": "CRASH:" + type(e).__name__, "reason_codes": [], "conjuncts": {}}
    to = st_auth(d)
    observed.append(("RECEIVED" if to == "REJECTED_SCHEMA" else "EVALUATING", to))
    return d
def commit(f, tok, binding, ev):
    c = f.gate.commit(tok, binding, ev); observed.append(("AUTHORIZED_UNUSED", st_commit(c))); return c
def binding_of(rec):
    return {k: rec[k] for k in ("request_digest", "canonical_target", "params_digest", "adapter_digest", "effect_type")}

# OP-POS: a valid request authorizes and its token commits once (the happy path exists).
f = T.GateFixture(); d = auth(f, f.request(), T.evidence_bundle())
pos_auth = d["decision"] == "ALLOW"
tok = d["authorization_receipt"]["commit_token"]["token_id"] if pos_auth else None
c1 = commit(f, tok, binding_of(d["authorization_receipt"]), {}) if pos_auth else None
check("OP-POS valid authorize -> AUTHORIZED_UNUSED and a single commit -> COMMITTED",
      bool(pos_auth and c1 and c1["outcome"] == "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME"))

# OP-R1: a denied snapshot must not later ALLOW; a FRESH request still evaluates (liveness).
f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request()
d1 = auth(f, copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle(authority=T.time_authority(sig="invalid")))
d2 = auth(f, copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle())                      # same snapshot
d3 = auth(f, f.request(nonce="c"*32), T.evidence_bundle())               # fresh, different snapshot
check("OP-R1 no allow-after-deny for a snapshot, and a fresh request still authorizes",
      d1["decision"].startswith("DENY") and d2["decision"] != "ALLOW" and d3["decision"] == "ALLOW")

# OP-R2: one-time redemption -- a second commit of the same token fails.
f = T.GateFixture(); d = auth(f, f.request(), T.evidence_bundle()); rec = d["authorization_receipt"]; tok = rec["commit_token"]["token_id"]
first = commit(f, tok, binding_of(rec), {}); second = commit(f, tok, binding_of(rec), {})
check("OP-R2 single-use redemption: first COMMITTED, second FAILED_NO_EFFECT",
      first["outcome"] == "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME" and second["outcome"] != "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME")

# OP-R6: cross-session isolation, and same-session widening still terminalizes (both directions).
f = T.GateFixture()
rA = f.request(effect="READ", nonce="a"*32); rA["session_id"] = "sA"; rA["request_id"] = "req-A"
auth(f, rA, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED")))
rB = f.request(effect="WRITE", nonce="b"*32); rB["session_id"] = "sB"; rB["request_id"] = "req-B"
dB = auth(f, rB, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="HEALTHY")))
rA2 = f.request(effect="WRITE", nonce="d"*32); rA2["session_id"] = "sA"; rA2["request_id"] = "req-A2"
dA2 = auth(f, rA2, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="HEALTHY")))
check("OP-R6 sessions isolated (B unaffected by A) AND same-session widening is terminal",
      "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING" not in dB["reason_codes"] and "sB" not in f.gate.state.terminal
      and "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING" in dA2["reason_codes"])

# OP-STRICT: a strict health drop with no actual narrowing must terminalize (E-NONSTRICT-NARROWING).
f2 = T.GateFixture(degraded_effects=["READ", "WRITE"], degraded_targets=["https://db.example.internal/records/"])
auth(f2, f2.request(effect="WRITE", nonce="a"*32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="HEALTHY")))
dS = auth(f2, f2.request(effect="WRITE", nonce="b"*32), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health="DEGRADED", epoch=8)))
check("OP-STRICT strict health drop without strict narrowing is terminal (E-NONSTRICT-NARROWING)",
      "E-NONSTRICT-NARROWING" in dS["reason_codes"])

# OP-R5: terminal recovery -- a valid independent amendment lifts; bad ones do not.
def amendment(session, adjs, cclass="LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE"):
    return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION,
            "correction_id": "cor-" + session, "correction_class": cclass,
            "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"prior-terminal"), "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED",
            "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops"},
            "session_id": session, "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain", "adjudicators": adjs,
            "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN",
            "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z", "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
def adj(ident, domain):
    return {"adjudicator_id": ident, "control_domain": domain, "signature": T.signature("valid", kid=ident)}
f = T.GateFixture(); f.gate.state.terminal["sX"] = "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING"
bad = f.gate.submit_amendment(amendment("sX", [adj("a1", "d1"), adj("a2", "d1")]))
dup = f.gate.submit_amendment(amendment("sX", [{"adjudicator_id":"a1","control_domain":"d1","signature":T.signature("valid",kid="same")},
                                               {"adjudicator_id":"a2","control_domain":"d2","signature":T.signature("valid",kid="same")}]))
wrongclass = f.gate.submit_amendment(amendment("sX", [adj("a1", "d1"), adj("a2", "d2")], cclass="SUPERSEDE"))
good = f.gate.submit_amendment(amendment("sX", [adj("a1", "d1"), adj("a2", "d2")]))
if good.get("accepted"):
    observed.append(("SESSION_TERMINAL", "EVALUATING"))
check("OP-R5 valid amendment recovers (SESSION_TERMINAL->EVALUATING); bad-domain / dup-sig / wrong-class rejected",
      good.get("accepted") is True and not bad.get("accepted") and not dup.get("accepted")
      and not wrongclass.get("accepted") and "sX" not in f.gate.state.terminal)

# OP-ABSORB: a request in an already-terminal session is refused (terminal is absorbing).
f = T.GateFixture(); f.gate.state.terminal["sT"] = "E-AUTHORITY-WIDENING"
rt = f.request(); rt["session_id"] = "sT"
dt = auth(f, rt, T.evidence_bundle())
check("OP-ABSORB request in a terminal session is refused (E-SESSION-TERMINAL)",
      "E-SESSION-TERMINAL" in dt["reason_codes"])

# OP-OD11: the subject-facing response conceals the denial regime (audit plane keeps it).
f = T.GateFixture()
dF = f.gate.authorize(f.request(), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(sig_status="invalid")))
ru = f.request(nonce="e"*32); ru["contract_digest"] = "sha256:" + "0"*64
dU = f.gate.authorize(ru, T.evidence_bundle())
sfF = f.gate.subject_facing(dF); sfU = f.gate.subject_facing(dU)
check("OP-OD11 subject-facing conceals DENY_FALSE vs DENY_UNKNOWN (audit plane retains it)",
      sfF == sfU and sfF.get("decision") == "DENIED" and dF["decision"] != dU["decision"])

# OP-REJECT: a structurally invalid request is a distinct REJECTED_SCHEMA state (spec S14.1),
# never conflated with a kernel-level DENY_UNKNOWN.
f = T.GateFixture(); rbad = f.request(); rbad["sequence"] = 2**60
drej = auth(f, rbad, T.evidence_bundle())
check("OP-REJECT structural admission failure is a distinct REJECTED_SCHEMA state",
      drej["decision"] == "REJECTED_SCHEMA" and st_auth(drej) == "REJECTED_SCHEMA")

# OP-DENY-PATHS: on EVERY denial path the identical request re-submitted must not ALLOW. The
# prior checker failed to exercise this -- P1 was an allow-after-deny on an early path it never
# drove -- so driving each path is what makes the no-DENY->ALLOW refinement non-vacuous.
import copy as _copy
_paths = []
f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request()                                   # (a) null evidence
_paths.append((auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), None), auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle())))
f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request(); r["contract_digest"] = "sha256:" + "0"*64   # (b) unknown contract
_paths.append((auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle()), auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle())))
f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request()                                   # (c) invalid witness signature
_bad = T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(sig_status="invalid"))
_paths.append((auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), _bad), auth(f, _copy.deepcopy(r), T.evidence_bundle())))
check("OP-DENY-PATHS every deny path is terminal for its snapshot (no allow-after-deny on ANY path)",
      all(a["decision"] != "ALLOW" and b["decision"] != "ALLOW" for a, b in _paths), "paths=%d" % len(_paths))

# NOTE ON STATE SCOPE: COMMITTING/VOID/EXPIRED/CONSUMED are internal token statuses, not
# decision-record outputs; the observable commit outcomes are COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME and
# FAILED_NO_EFFECT. INDETERMINATE (unknown remote outcome) and QUARANTINED (artifact plane)
# are outside the reference gate's commit path and are declared OUT OF MODEL here.

# REFINEMENT: every transition observed on the real gate is a permitted Section-14 transition.
bad_edges = [(a, b) for (a, b) in observed if b not in PERMITTED.get(a, set())]
check("REFINE every observed executable transition is permitted by Section 14",
      not bad_edges, "observed=%d illegal=%d" % (len(observed), len(bad_edges)))
check("REFINE no DENIED_*->AUTHORIZED_UNUSED edge ever occurred on the executable",
      not any(a in ("DENIED_FALSE", "DENIED_UNKNOWN") and b == "AUTHORIZED_UNUSED" for (a, b) in observed))


def _check_record_kernel_consistency(gate):
    """GPT-04 + F (GPT-05/KIMI-5/6): on every KERNEL-EVALUATED decision record, assert
    recorded_decision == kernel_decision(recorded_conjuncts). Records are classified at emission by the
    explicit `kernel_evaluated` flag: kernel ALLOW/DENY, issuance failures (collision/mint), and aborts
    are kernel-evaluated (and must be consistent); protocol/admission denials (nonce replay, session
    terminal, structural reject, reentrant refusal) are kernel_evaluated=False and exempt -- their
    decision is a protocol classification, not a kernel one, so the universal claim is honestly scoped."""
    from bssg_gate import kernel_decision
    bad = []
    for rec in gate.state.records:
        if rec.get("record_type") != "bssg.decision":
            continue
        if rec.get("kernel_evaluated") is not True:
            continue
        cj = rec.get("conjuncts")
        if not isinstance(cj, dict):
            bad.append((rec.get("decision"), "NON-DICT-CONJUNCTS", cj)); continue
        expected, _ = kernel_decision(dict(cj))
        if expected != rec.get("decision"):
            bad.append((rec.get("decision"), expected, cj))
    return bad

# F: drive collision, mint-abort, nonce-replay, and reentrant-refusal traces, then assert kernel
# consistency over EVERY kernel-evaluated record across all of them (not only the collision fixture).
import test_bssg_gate as _KT
_kf = _KT.GateFixture()
_ka1 = _kf.gate.authorize(_kf.request(nonce="a" * 32), _KT.evidence_bundle())
_ktok = _ka1["authorization_receipt"]["commit_token"]["token_id"]
_kf.gate.new_token = lambda: _ktok
_kd2 = _kf.gate.authorize(_kf.request(nonce="b" * 32), _KT.evidence_bundle())        # collision
def _mint_boom():
    raise RuntimeError("mint fault")
_kf.gate.new_token = _mint_boom
_kd3 = _kf.gate.authorize(_kf.request(nonce="c" * 32), _KT.evidence_bundle())        # mint abort (issuance failure)
_rf = _KT.GateFixture()
_r_a = _rf.request(nonce="e" * 32); _r_a["request_id"] = "rid-A"
_r_b = _rf.request(nonce="e" * 32); _r_b["request_id"] = "rid-B"   # same nonce, distinct snapshot
_rf.gate.authorize(_r_a, _KT.evidence_bundle())                     # consumes the nonce
_rf.gate.authorize(_r_b, _KT.evidence_bundle())                     # E-NONCE-REPLAY: DENY_FALSE, all-UNKNOWN
# the replay record is kernel_evaluated=False and INCONSISTENT (DENY_FALSE vs kernel(all-UNKNOWN)=
# DENY_UNKNOWN); it MUST be exempted, which makes the scoping load-bearing rather than vacuous.
_rep = _rf.gate.state.records[-1]
_replay_exempt_inconsistent = (
    _rep.get("kernel_evaluated") is False
    and "E-NONCE-REPLAY" in _rep.get("reason_codes", [])
    and G.kernel_decision(dict(_rep["conjuncts"]))[0] != _rep["decision"])
# F/GPT-06 (pass-18): drive an ACTUAL reentrant refusal (a token factory that re-enters authorize during
# mint of the outer call) and assert the pre-admission refusal is classified kernel_evaluated=False with
# all-UNKNOWN conjuncts -- so code, classification, and this trace agree.
_ef = _KT.GateFixture()
_nested_holder = {}
def _reentrant_factory():
    _nested_holder["rec"] = _ef.gate.authorize(_ef.request(nonce="9" * 32), _KT.evidence_bundle())
    return "tok-outer-01"
_ef.gate.new_token = _reentrant_factory
_ef.gate.authorize(_ef.request(nonce="8" * 32), _KT.evidence_bundle())
_nested = _nested_holder.get("rec", {})
_reentrant_ok = (
    _nested.get("reason_codes") == ["E-REENTRANT-CALL"]
    and _nested.get("kernel_evaluated") is False
    and all(v == "UNKNOWN" for v in _nested.get("conjuncts", {"x": "TRUE"}).values()))
_kbad = (_check_record_kernel_consistency(_kf.gate)
         + _check_record_kernel_consistency(_rf.gate)
         + _check_record_kernel_consistency(_ef.gate))
_issuance_ok = _kd2.get("issuance_status") == "FAILED_NO_EFFECT" and _kd3.get("issuance_status") == "FAILED_NO_EFFECT"
check("REFINE every kernel-evaluated record satisfies decision == kernel_decision(conjuncts); protocol records (replay, reentrant) exempt and load-bearing",
      not _kbad and _issuance_ok and _replay_exempt_inconsistent and _reentrant_ok,
      "records=%d inconsistent=%d collision=%s mint_abort=%s replay_exempt=%s reentrant_false=%s" % (
          len(_kf.gate.state.records) + len(_rf.gate.state.records) + len(_ef.gate.state.records), len(_kbad),
          _kd2.get("issuance_status"), _kd3.get("issuance_status"), _replay_exempt_inconsistent, _reentrant_ok))

# ===========================================================================
# BOUNDED AUTHORITY-INTRODUCTION MODEL CHECK (V29 -- Axiom 3 / R_may closure).
# The CE corpus enumerates hand-picked schedules (R_declared); GPT-P30-01 lived
# in the schedule NO ONE enumerated (R_may). This check drives the REAL gate over
# EVERY schedule of bounded length from a small op alphabet and asserts the
# structural invariant the effect bypass violated: after a fixed healthy
# establishment under contract C1 ({READ,WRITE}), no ALLOW on ANY schedule may
# authorize an effect OUTSIDE that established ceiling -- no authorize/reject/lift
# sequence can introduce authority (DELETE) the session never legitimately held.
# GRANTED is fixed independently of the gate's contract_ceiling internals, so the
# check actually TESTS them rather than restating them. A locally-green but
# structurally-unjustified "fix" fails HERE, over all schedules, not five rounds
# later at a seat. On the poisoned gate it FAILS; on the repaired gate it PASSES.
# ===========================================================================
def _reg_c2(fx):
    b = T.contract_body(fx.policy_digest)
    b["allowed_effects"] = ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"]
    b["effect_registry"]["DELETE"] = {"adapter_digest": T.ADAPTER_DIGEST}
    b["degraded_effects"] = ["READ"]
    d = G.digest(b); fx.gate.contracts[d] = {"body": copy.deepcopy(b), "revoked": False}
    return d
def _amd_ab(session, cid):
    a = lambda i, dm: {"adjudicator_id": i, "control_domain": dm, "signature": T.signature("valid", kid=i)}
    return {"record_type": "bssg.correction-event", "spec_version": G.SPEC_VERSION, "correction_id": cid,
            "correction_class": "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE", "supersedes": G.digest_bytes(b"prior"),
            "reason_code": "C-TERMINAL-REVIEWED", "issued_by": {"authority_id": "ops-1", "control_domain": "ops-domain"},
            "session_id": session, "subject_control_domain": "subject-domain",
            "adjudicators": [a("adj-1", "audit-domain"), a("adj-2", "ops-domain")],
            "descendants": [], "descendant_closure_status": "UNKNOWN", "issued_at": "2026-07-25T12:01:00Z",
            "signature": T.signature("valid", kid="ops-key")}
_GRANTED = frozenset({"READ", "WRITE"})   # C1 healthy ceiling = the authority legitimately established
_GRANTED_TARGETS = frozenset({"https://db.example.internal/records/"})  # V30 K32-02: track target scopes too
# V31 K33-02 FIX: alphabet expanded with ALLOWed-degraded C2 READ (the K33-01 vector) and C2 READ HEALTHY.
# The prior alphabet omitted any legitimately-ALLOWed degraded C2 operation, so the allowed-degraded
# ceiling-poison schedule was outside the checked space. Also: C2W registers a broader target scope
# ({records/, admin/}) while retaining degraded={records/} only, so target-scope introduction is exercised.
_OPS = [("A", "C1", "READ", "HEALTHY"), ("A", "C1", "WRITE", "DEGRADED"),
        ("A", "C2", "DELETE", "HEALTHY"), ("A", "C2", "DELETE", "DEGRADED"),
        ("A", "C2", "READ", "DEGRADED"),   # K33-01: the allowed-degraded poison vector
        ("A", "C2", "READ", "HEALTHY"),     # K33-01: broader-contract healthy read
        ("L",)]
_K = 4  # V30 GLM-F2/K32-02: raised from 3; retained at 4 with expanded 7-op alphabet
_bmc_sched = 0; _bmc_allows = 0; _bmc_violation = None
for _len in range(1, _K + 1):
    for _sched in product(_OPS, repeat=_len):
        fx = T.GateFixture(); c2 = _reg_c2(fx)
        _ep = 7; _seq = 11; _nz = 0; _lift = 0
        _est = fx.gate.authorize(fx.request(effect="WRITE", nonce=("%032x" % _nz)),
                                 T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(epoch=_ep, sequence=_seq), epoch=5))
        _nz += 1; _ep += 1; _seq += 1; _bmc_sched += 1
        if _est.get("decision") != "ALLOW":
            continue
        for _op in _sched:
            if _op[0] == "L":
                fx.gate.submit_amendment(_amd_ab("sess-0001", "cor-%d" % _lift)); _lift += 1; continue
            _, _which, _eff, _h = _op
            _req = (T.effect_request(c2, fx.policy_digest, effect=_eff, nonce=("%032x" % _nz))
                    if _which == "C2" else fx.request(effect=_eff, nonce=("%032x" % _nz)))
            try:
                _d = fx.gate.authorize(_req, T.evidence_bundle(receipt=T.witness_receipt(health=_h, epoch=_ep, sequence=_seq), epoch=5))
            except Exception as _e:
                _d = {"decision": "CRASH:" + type(_e).__name__}
            _nz += 1; _ep += 1; _seq += 1
            if _d.get("decision") == "ALLOW":
                _bmc_allows += 1
                # V30 K32-02 FIX: check BOTH effect and target-scope introduction
                _auth_snap = _d.get("authority_snapshot")
                _allowed_effs = frozenset(_auth_snap.get("effects", [])) if isinstance(_auth_snap, dict) else frozenset()
                _allowed_tgts = frozenset(_auth_snap.get("target_scopes", [])) if isinstance(_auth_snap, dict) else frozenset()
                if _eff not in _GRANTED or not _allowed_effs.issubset(_GRANTED) or not _allowed_tgts.issubset(_GRANTED_TARGETS):
                    _bmc_violation = (_sched, _eff); break
        if _bmc_violation: break
    if _bmc_violation: break
check("BOUNDED-AUTH-INTRO: over all schedules (len<=%d, %d-op alphabet) no ALLOW authorizes an effect or target outside the established healthy ceiling (Axiom-3/R_may closure)" % (_K, len(_OPS)),
      _bmc_violation is None, "schedules=%d allows=%d%s" % (_bmc_sched, _bmc_allows, "" if _bmc_violation is None else " VIOLATION sched=%r eff=%s" % _bmc_violation))

print()
if fail:
    print("MODEL CHECK: FAIL -- %d: %s" % (len(fail), ", ".join(fail))); sys.exit(1)
print("MODEL CHECK: PASS -- kernel(243) + antitone(%d) + bounded-auth-intro(%d schedules) + operational refinement on the executable" % (contracts, _bmc_sched))
sys.exit(0)


======== bssg_attest.py ========
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Packet-sample digest (NOT an execution proof).

HONEST SCOPE (revised pass-20 after finding K19-A1). Earlier rounds framed this value as an
"execution-bound attestation" that "cannot be produced without running the gate." That framing was
wrong and is withdrawn. A V18 auditor showed the trace is ~94% constant across nonces: only a thin
digest chain (per-request request_digest, receipt_id, receipt digests) varies, and those are
reproducible from ONE sampled trace plus a reimplementation of the digest function -- WITHOUT executing
the gate's decision logic. An output-hash over deterministic operations fundamentally cannot bind
execution against a party who can reproduce the outputs, so no amount of iterating this file fixes it.

What this value IS: a deterministic function of (the packet bytes, the nonce) that a seat can compute
after receiving the packet. Combined with the manifest challenge, it shows the seat had the exact
bytes. It is NOT evidence that the seat executed the gate, and it does NOT bind model identity.

What actually governs (SHV D6 / Bayyinah Audit Framework): certification rests on INDEPENDENT
REPRODUCTION -- each non-producer, different-vendor seat re-derives every load-bearing finding on the
canonical bytes, and the producer reproduces each finding before accepting it. That multi-chain
reproduction, not this hash, is the evidence. Seats should return verbatim command outputs and
reproducers; a bare matching hash with no reproduced findings carries little weight.

Usage:  python3 bssg_attest.py <NONCE>    (prints EXECUTION_ATTESTATION: <hex>; kept for dispatch
        continuity and byte-binding, with the scope above -- it is a packet-sample digest, not a proof)
"""
import sys, hashlib, json
import bssg_gate as G
import test_bssg_gate as T

_CANON = dict(sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str)


def run(nonce):
    def n(i):
        return hashlib.sha256((nonce + "|" + str(i)).encode()).hexdigest()[:32]
    trace = []
    rec = lambda label, obj: trace.append([label, obj])

    # 1. valid authorize -> single commit -> double-spend. Record the FULL ALLOW decision record
    #    (nonce-dependent request_digest, gate-minted receipt_id + token binding + authority fields)
    #    and both commit execution records, not just the decision labels.
    f = T.GateFixture()
    d = f.gate.authorize(f.request(nonce=n(1)), T.evidence_bundle())
    rec("auth", d)
    if d["decision"] == "ALLOW":
        rc = d["authorization_receipt"]
        b = {k: rc[k] for k in ("request_digest", "canonical_target", "params_digest", "adapter_digest", "effect_type")}
        rec("commit1", f.gate.commit(rc["commit_token"]["token_id"], b, {}))
        rec("commit2", f.gate.commit(rc["commit_token"]["token_id"], b, {}))

    # 2. allow-after-deny probe on the early (null-evidence) path -- full records.
    f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request(nonce=n(2))
    rec("deny", f.gate.authorize(dict(r), None))
    rec("reauth", f.gate.authorize(dict(r), T.evidence_bundle()))

    # 3. out-of-range integer (totality) -- full record or crash marker.
    f = T.GateFixture(); r = f.request(nonce=n(3)); r["sequence"] = 2 ** 60
    try:
        rec("oor_int", f.gate.authorize(r, T.evidence_bundle()))
    except Exception as e:
        rec("oor_int", "CRASH:" + type(e).__name__)

    # 4. schema-invalid witness receipt -- full record.
    f = T.GateFixture(); w = T.witness_receipt(); w["x_undeclared"] = "1"
    rec("bad_witness", f.gate.authorize(f.request(nonce=n(4)), T.evidence_bundle(receipt=w)))

    # 5. token-factory collision -> kernel-consistent issuance failure -- full record (exercises the
    #    ALLOW-shaped FAILED_NO_EFFECT issuance path, a gate-logic outcome).
    f = T.GateFixture()
    a = f.gate.authorize(f.request(nonce=n(5)), T.evidence_bundle())
    f.gate.new_token = lambda: a["authorization_receipt"]["commit_token"]["token_id"]
    rec("collision", f.gate.authorize(f.request(nonce=n(6)), T.evidence_bundle()))

    payload = json.dumps(trace, **_CANON)
    return hashlib.sha256((nonce + "|" + payload).encode()).hexdigest()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    nonce = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ""
    print("EXECUTION_ATTESTATION:", run(nonce))


======== verify_bssg_v0_1.py ========
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
BSSG v0.1 DRAFT package verifier.

Runs twelve gates over the package directory it lives in:

  G1  file parity and required-file presence
  G2  JSON Schema 2020-12 validity and closure discipline for the six schemas
  G3  test suite execution
  G4  citation URL extraction sanity (every cited URL must appear in the
      specification's reference list; no invented hosts)
  G5  prohibited effectiveness-language scan
  G6  normative-term scan (BCP 14 boilerplate and uppercase usage)
  G7  dash scan (no em dash or en dash characters in source files)
  G8  version and status parity
  G9  SHA-256 manifest check against SHA256SUMS
  G10 unresolved open-decision report
  G11 X4/X7/X8 mechanical-checker exercise (negative + positive vectors, real
      gate-record binding, and tamper-detection against vacuity)
  G12 claim-substrate consistency (load-bearing REPAIR_STATUS claims re-verified
      against runtime behavior; every cited counterexample id must exist)

Exit status is 0 when no gate FAILS. WARN does not fail the package: an
unresolved design decision is an honest state, an inconsistent package is not.

This verifier makes no claim about the effectiveness, security, or correctness
of anything it verifies. It checks package self-consistency only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys

# V27 breadth-bound: a wide-holding record (epoch 0) = the pre-degradation authority a realistic honest
# recovery restores to. Prepending it to a synthetic honest re-widen makes the stream a non-fragment.
_HELD = {"session_id": "s", "authority_epoch": 0, "authority_snapshot": {"effects": ["READ", "WRITE", "DELETE"], "target_scopes": ["a"]}}

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SPEC = "BSSG_v0_1_CANONICAL_SPEC_DRAFT.pplx.md"
SCHEMA_DIR = os.path.join(HERE, "schemas")

REQUIRED_FILES = [
    SPEC,
    "BSSG_v0_1_MODEL_COUNCIL_SYNTHESIS.pplx.md",
    "BSSG_v0_1_DECISION_REGISTER.md",
    "README.md",
    "REQUIREMENTS_TRACEABILITY.md",
    "ATTACK_AND_FALSIFIER_REGISTER.md",
    "OPEN_DECISIONS.md",
    "bssg_gate.py",
    "test_bssg_gate.py",
    "verify_bssg_v0_1.py",
]

REQUIRED_SCHEMAS = [
    "effect-request.schema.json",
    "authorization-receipt.schema.json",
    "witness-receipt.schema.json",
    "execution-receipt.schema.json",
    "correction-event.schema.json",
    "lineage-record.schema.json",
]

# Phrases that may not appear outside the registry. Lines carrying the marker
# token below are skipped by the scanner, including these very lines.
PROHIBITED_PATTERNS = [
    r"prevents harm",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"makes agents safe",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"reduces unauthorized effects",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"outperform",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"proven secure",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"guarantees safety",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"is validated",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"prevents equivocation",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"preserves capability(?! under evidence)",  # prohibition #8: BARE form only (conditional allowed)
    r"guarantees security",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"guarantees correctness",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"guarantees safe",  # prohibited-phrase-registry (broadened beyond 'guarantees safety')
    r"\bis safe\b",  # prohibited-phrase-registry (unqualified affirmative safety claim; negated disclaimers reworded)
    r"tolerates witness failure",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"state of the art",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"industry leading",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"battle tested",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
    r"production ready",  # prohibited-phrase-registry
]
SKIP_MARKER = "prohibited-phrase-registry"
# Audit / producer meta-documents legitimately NAME the prohibited phrases while recording
# findings; the Section 1.3 prohibition targets BSSG documents, README, and the deposit
# abstract, not the audit trail. G5 exempts these from the effectiveness-language scan.
AUDIT_META = (
    "DECISION_REGISTER", "MODEL_COUNCIL_SYNTHESIS", "PRODUCER", "ATTACK_AND_FALSIFIER",
    "REQUIREMENTS_TRACEABILITY", "OPEN_DECISIONS", "REPAIR_STATUS",
    "bssg_counterexamples", "bssg_model_check", "verify_bssg",
)

NORMATIVE_TERMS = [
    "MUST NOT",
    "MUST",
    "SHALL NOT",
    "SHALL",
    "SHOULD NOT",
    "SHOULD",
    "REQUIRED",
    "RECOMMENDED",
    "MAY",
    "OPTIONAL",
]

DASHES = {
    "\u2013": "en dash",
    "\u2014": "em dash",
    "\u2012": "figure dash",
    "\u2015": "horizontal bar",
    "\u2212": "minus sign",
}

MD_LINK_RE = re.compile(r"\[[^\]]*\]\((https?://[^)\s]+)\)")
BARE_URL_RE = re.compile(r"(?<![(\w])(https?://[^\s`\"'<>)\]]+)")

results = []


def record(gate, status, message):
    results.append((gate, status, message))
    print("%-5s %-6s %s" % (gate, status, message))


# Dispatch/transport wrappers are not packet content; the verifier ignores them so it
# runs cleanly whether or not an audit brief or reconstruction helper travels alongside.
DISPATCH_ARTIFACTS = ("AUDIT_BRIEF", "reconstruct.py", "PRODUCER_KEPT")

def source_files():
    out = []
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(HERE):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ("__pycache__", ".git")]
        for name in files:
            if not name.endswith((".md", ".py", ".json")):
                continue
            if name.startswith("schema__") or any(a in name for a in DISPATCH_ARTIFACTS):
                continue
            out.append(os.path.join(root, name))
    return sorted(out)


def read(path):
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return handle.read()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G1 file parity
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g1():
    missing = [f for f in REQUIRED_FILES if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(HERE, f))]
    for schema in REQUIRED_SCHEMAS:
        if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, schema)):
            missing.append("schemas/" + schema)
    if missing:
        record("G1", "FAIL", "missing required files: %s" % ", ".join(missing))
        return
    present = sorted(os.listdir(SCHEMA_DIR))
    extra = [f for f in present if f not in REQUIRED_SCHEMAS]
    if extra:
        record("G1", "FAIL", "unexpected files in schemas/: %s" % ", ".join(extra))
        return
    record(
        "G1",
        "PASS",
        "all %d required files and exactly %d schemas present"
        % (len(REQUIRED_FILES), len(REQUIRED_SCHEMAS)),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G2 schema validity
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g2():
    try:
        import jsonschema
    except Exception:
        record("G2", "WARN", "jsonschema not installed; structural check only")
        jsonschema = None
    problems = []
    for name in REQUIRED_SCHEMAS:
        path = os.path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, name)
        try:
            schema = json.loads(read(path))
        except Exception as exc:
            problems.append("%s: not parseable (%s)" % (name, exc))
            continue
        if schema.get("$schema") != "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema":
            problems.append("%s: $schema is not the 2020-12 dialect" % name)
        schema_id = schema.get("$id", "")
        if not schema_id.startswith("urn:bssg:schema:0.1:"):
            problems.append("%s: $id is not a urn:bssg:schema:0.1 identifier" % name)
        if schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False:
            problems.append("%s: root object is not closed" % name)
        if "DRAFT" not in json.dumps(schema):
            problems.append("%s: no DRAFT status marker in the description" % name)
        if jsonschema is not None:
            try:
                jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
            except Exception as exc:
                problems.append("%s: invalid schema (%s)" % (name, exc))
    # the frozen kernel must not gain a sixth conjunct
    auth = json.loads(read(os.path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, "authorization-receipt.schema.json")))
    conjuncts = auth["$defs"]["conjuncts"]["properties"]
    expected = {
        "ContractMatch",
        "EffectTypeValid",
        "TargetAllowed",
        "WitnessHealthy",
        "EvidenceCurrent",
    }
    if set(conjuncts) != expected:
        problems.append("authorization-receipt: conjunct set is not the frozen five")
    if problems:
        record("G2", "FAIL", "; ".join(problems))
    else:
        record("G2", "PASS", "six schemas valid, closed, DRAFT-marked, five conjuncts")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G3 tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g3():
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "test_bssg_gate", "-v"],
        cwd=HERE,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    output = proc.stdout + proc.stderr
    match = re.search(r"Ran (\d+) tests", output)
    count = int(match.group(1)) if match else -1
    kernel = len(set(re.findall(r"test_kernel_\d{3}_[TFU_]+", output)))
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        tail = output.strip().splitlines()[-15:]
        record("G3", "FAIL", "tests failed (%d run): %s" % (count, " | ".join(tail)))
        return
    if kernel < 243:
        record("G3", "FAIL", "kernel truth states enumerated: %d, expected 243" % kernel)
        return
    record("G3", "PASS", "%d tests passed, including %d kernel truth states" % (count, kernel))
    return count


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G4 citation sanity
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g4():
    spec_text = read(os.path.join(HERE, SPEC))
    refs_section = spec_text.split("## 32. References", 1)
    if len(refs_section) != 2:
        record("G4", "FAIL", "specification has no reference section")
        return
    allow = set(BARE_URL_RE.findall(refs_section[1]))
    allow.add("https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema")
    if not allow:
        record("G4", "FAIL", "reference section contains no URLs")
        return
    problems = []
    total = 0
    for path in source_files():
        if not path.endswith(".md"):
            continue
        text = read(path)
        if path.endswith(SPEC):
            text = text.split("## 32. References", 1)[0]
        urls = set(MD_LINK_RE.findall(text)) | set(BARE_URL_RE.findall(text))
        for url in urls:
            total += 1
            clean = url.rstrip(".,;:")
            if clean not in allow:
                problems.append("%s cites unlisted URL %s" % (os.path.basename(path), clean))
    bad_shape = [u for u in allow if " " in u or u.count("://") != 1]
    if bad_shape:
        problems.append("malformed reference URLs: %s" % bad_shape)
    if problems:
        record("G4", "FAIL", "; ".join(sorted(set(problems))[:10]))
    else:
        record(
            "G4",
            "PASS",
            "%d citation occurrences, all resolvable to the %d listed references"
            % (total, len(allow)),
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G5 prohibited language
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g5():
    hits = []
    for path in source_files():
        if any(m in os.path.basename(path) for m in AUDIT_META):
            continue  # meta-docs discuss the prohibited terms; they do not assert them
        for number, line in enumerate(read(path).splitlines(), 1):
            if SKIP_MARKER in line.lower():
                continue
            if "PROHIBITED-PHRASE-REGISTRY" in line:
                continue
            lowered = line.lower()
            for pattern in PROHIBITED_PATTERNS:
                if re.search(pattern, lowered):
                    hits.append("%s:%d %s" % (os.path.basename(path), number, pattern))
    if hits:
        record("G5", "FAIL", "prohibited effectiveness language: %s" % "; ".join(hits[:10]))
    else:
        record("G5", "PASS", "no prohibited effectiveness language outside the registry")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G6 normative terms
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g6():
    spec_text = read(os.path.join(HERE, SPEC))
    problems = []
    if "RFC 2119" not in spec_text or "RFC 8174" not in spec_text:
        problems.append("BCP 14 boilerplate does not cite both RFC 2119 and RFC 8174")
    if "when, and only when, they appear in all capitals" not in spec_text:
        problems.append("BCP 14 capitalization clause missing")
    counts = {}
    for term in NORMATIVE_TERMS:
        counts[term] = len(re.findall(r"\b%s\b" % term, spec_text))
    for term in ("MUST", "MUST NOT", "SHALL", "SHOULD", "MAY"):
        if counts[term] == 0:
            problems.append("normative term %s never used" % term)
    ambiguous = re.findall(r"\b(Must|Shall|Should|May not)\b\s+(?:be|not|have)", spec_text)
    _counts = ("BCP 14 boilerplate present; MUST=%d MUST NOT=%d SHALL=%d SHOULD=%d MAY=%d"
               % (counts["MUST"], counts["MUST NOT"], counts["SHALL"], counts["SHOULD"], counts["MAY"]))
    if problems:
        record("G6", "FAIL", "; ".join(problems))
    elif ambiguous:
        record("G6", "WARN", "mixed-case normative-looking terms: %s; %s" % (set(ambiguous), _counts))
    else:
        record("G6", "PASS", _counts)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G7 dashes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g7():
    hits = []
    for path in source_files():
        text = read(path)
        for char, label in DASHES.items():
            if char in text:
                index = text.index(char)
                hits.append("%s contains %s at offset %d" % (os.path.basename(path), label, index))
    if hits:
        record("G7", "FAIL", "; ".join(hits[:10]))
    else:
        record("G7", "PASS", "no em dash, en dash, or related characters in source files")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G8 version and status parity
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g8():
    problems = []
    gate_src = read(os.path.join(HERE, "bssg_gate.py"))
    if 'SPEC_VERSION = "0.1"' not in gate_src:
        problems.append("bssg_gate.py SPEC_VERSION is not 0.1")
    for name in REQUIRED_SCHEMAS:
        schema = json.loads(read(os.path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, name)))
        blob = json.dumps(schema)
        if '"const": "0.1"' not in blob and "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:" not in blob:
            problems.append("%s does not pin spec version 0.1" % name)
    for name in REQUIRED_FILES:
        if not name.endswith(".md"):
            continue
        text = read(os.path.join(HERE, name))
        if "DRAFT" not in text:
            problems.append("%s does not carry a DRAFT marker" % name)
        if "NOT CARRIED" not in text:
            problems.append("%s does not carry a NOT CARRIED marker" % name)
    spec_text = read(os.path.join(HERE, SPEC))
    for phrase in (
        "NOT PUBLICATION-READY",
        "Bilal Syed Arfeen",
        "HALT-WITH-FINDINGS",
        "CONDITIONAL APPROVAL",
    ):
        if phrase not in spec_text:
            problems.append("specification is missing required phrase: %s" % phrase)
    if "council approved" in spec_text.lower() or "approved by the council" in spec_text.lower():
        problems.append("specification claims council approval")
    if problems:
        record("G8", "FAIL", "; ".join(problems))
    else:
        record("G8", "PASS", "version 0.1 parity and DRAFT / NOT CARRIED status intact")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G9 manifest
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gate_g9():
    manifest_path = os.path.join(HERE, "SHA256SUMS")
    if not os.path.isfile(manifest_path):
        record("G9", "WARN", "SHA256SUMS absent; run before packaging")
        return
    problems = []
    listed = set()
    for line in read(manifest_path).splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        parts = line.split(None, 1)
        if len(parts) != 2:
            problems.append("malformed manifest line: %s" % line)
            continue
        expected, name = parts[0], parts[1].lstrip("*").strip()
        listed.add(name)
        path = os.path.join(HERE, name)
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
