======== authorization-receipt.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:authorization-receipt",
  "title": "BSSG authorization receipt (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "Issued by the decision point when and only when all five frozen conjuncts recomputed to TRUE. The receipt binds the decision to the exact request bytes, contract and policy digests, witness receipt, evidence epoch, authority epoch, nonce, and expiry, and carries a one-time commit token. The receipt is NOT permission to execute later under different conditions: the commit protocol revalidates. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "receipt_id",
    "request_digest",
    "request_id",
    "subject_id",
    "session_id",
    "contract_digest",
    "policy_digest",
    "witness_receipt_digest",
    "evidence_epoch",
    "witness_epoch",
    "authority_epoch",
    "authority_snapshot",
    "decision",
    "conjuncts",
    "reason_codes",
    "nonce",
    "effect_type",
    "canonical_target",
    "params_digest",
    "adapter_digest",
    "commit_token",
    "issued_at_epoch_seconds",
    "signature"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": { "const": "bssg.authorization-receipt" },
    "spec_version": { "const": "0.1" },
    "receipt_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "request_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "request_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "subject_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "session_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "contract_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "policy_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "witness_receipt_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "evidence_epoch": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
    "witness_epoch": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
    "authority_epoch": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch",
      "description": "Monotone non-decreasing. A commit whose authority epoch differs from the epoch bound here MUST fail."
    },
    "authority_snapshot": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["effects", "target_scopes"],
      "properties": {
        "effects": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
        "target_scopes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
      },
      "description": "The authority ceiling in force at decision time. Degradation of witness health MUST NOT enlarge this set."
    },
    "decision": { "const": "ALLOW" },
    "conjuncts": { "$ref": "#/$defs/conjuncts" },
    "reason_codes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/reasonCode" },
      "maxItems": 0,
      "description": "An ALLOW receipt carries no reason codes. DENY outcomes are recorded in a decision record, not in an authorization receipt."
    },
    "nonce": { "$ref": "#/$defs/nonce" },
    "effect_type": { "type": "string" },
    "canonical_target": { "type": "string" },
    "params_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "adapter_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "commit_token": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["token_id", "one_time", "expires_at_epoch_seconds"],
      "properties": {
        "token_id": { "type": "string", "minLength": 8, "maxLength": 128 },
        "one_time": { "const": true },
        "expires_at_epoch_seconds": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 }
      }
    },
    "issued_at_epoch_seconds": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 },
    "signature": { "$ref": "#/$defs/signature" }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "identifier": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$" },
    "digest": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$" },
    "nonce": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{32}$" },
    "epoch": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 9007199254740991 },
    "truthValue": { "enum": ["TRUE", "FALSE", "UNKNOWN"] },
    "conjuncts": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "ContractMatch",
        "EffectTypeValid",
        "TargetAllowed",
        "WitnessHealthy",
        "EvidenceCurrent"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "ContractMatch": { "$ref": "#/$defs/truthValue" },
        "EffectTypeValid": { "$ref": "#/$defs/truthValue" },
        "TargetAllowed": { "$ref": "#/$defs/truthValue" },
        "WitnessHealthy": { "$ref": "#/$defs/truthValue" },
        "EvidenceCurrent": { "$ref": "#/$defs/truthValue" }
      },
      "description": "Exactly the five frozen conjuncts. No sixth conjunct may appear. Commit-time revalidation is a protocol obligation recorded in the execution receipt, not an additional conjunct."
    },
    "reasonCode": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^E-[A-Z0-9-]{2,64}$" },
    "signature": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["alg", "kid", "envelope", "canonicalization", "verification_status"],
      "properties": {
        "alg": { "type": "string" },
        "kid": { "type": "string" },
        "envelope": { "enum": ["detached-json", "COSE_Sign1", "COSE_Sign"] },
        "canonicalization": { "const": "BSSG-CANON-1" },
        "signature_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "cose_sign1_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "verification_status": {
          "enum": [
            "verified_by_injected_verifier",
            "rejected_by_injected_verifier",
            "unknown",
            "not_performed_by_reference_gate"
          ],
          "description": "The reference implementation models signature verification through an injected verifier. It does not establish key custody, key distribution, or trust-root provenance. 'verified_by_injected_verifier' is a statement about a callable, not about a key."
        }
      }
    }
  }
}


======== correction-event.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:correction-event",
  "title": "BSSG correction event (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "A correction is a NEW record deriving from a prior record. Records are never edited in place. A correction that widens authority, or that lifts a terminal state, MUST carry at least two adjudicators in distinct control domains, neither of which is the subject's domain. Descendant closure that has not been established MUST be reported as PARTIAL or UNKNOWN and MUST NOT be reported as COMPLETE. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "correction_id",
    "correction_class",
    "supersedes",
    "reason_code",
    "issued_by",
    "issued_at",
    "descendants",
    "descendant_closure_status",
    "signature"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": { "const": "bssg.correction-event" },
    "spec_version": { "const": "0.1" },
    "correction_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "correction_class": {
      "enum": ["SUPERSEDE", "INVALIDATE", "AMEND_AUTHORITY", "LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE"]
    },
    "supersedes": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/digest",
      "description": "Digest of the corrected record. The corrected record remains in the log; evidence that justified the original decision MUST NOT be destroyed by the correction."
    },
    "reason_code": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^C-[A-Z0-9-]{2,64}$" },
    "issued_by": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["authority_id", "control_domain"],
      "properties": {
        "authority_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
        "control_domain": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 }
      }
    },
    "session_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "subject_control_domain": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
    "adjudicators": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 2,
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "required": ["adjudicator_id", "control_domain", "signature"],
        "properties": {
          "adjudicator_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
          "control_domain": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
          "reproduction_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
          "signature": { "$ref": "#/$defs/signature" }
        }
      },
      "description": "REQUIRED for AMEND_AUTHORITY and LIFT_TERMINAL_STATE. Distinctness of control domains is checked structurally; actual independence of those domains is an assumption the reference implementation cannot establish."
    },
    "descendants": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
      "description": "Recorded descendants known to be affected by this correction."
    },
    "unreachable_descendants": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
      "description": "Descendants known to exist but not reachable for correction. A non-empty list forbids a COMPLETE closure claim."
    },
    "descendant_closure_status": {
      "enum": ["COMPLETE", "PARTIAL", "UNKNOWN"],
      "description": "UNKNOWN is a first-class value. It MUST NOT be smoothed into COMPLETE."
    },
    "issued_at": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" },
    "effective_from": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" },
    "notes": { "type": "string" },
    "signature": { "$ref": "#/$defs/signature" }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "identifier": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$" },
    "digest": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$" },
    "timestamp": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z$"
    },
    "signature": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["alg", "kid", "envelope", "canonicalization"],
      "properties": {
        "alg": { "type": "string" },
        "kid": { "type": "string" },
        "envelope": { "enum": ["detached-json", "COSE_Sign1", "COSE_Sign"] },
        "canonicalization": { "const": "BSSG-CANON-1" },
        "signature_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "cose_sign1_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "verification_status": {
          "enum": [
            "verified_by_injected_verifier",
            "rejected_by_injected_verifier",
            "unknown",
            "not_performed_by_reference_gate"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}


======== effect-request.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:effect-request",
  "title": "BSSG effect request (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "A request by a subject for a consequential effect. This record carries ONLY raw evidence and declarations. It MUST NOT carry any derived authorization fact: no decision, no conjunct value, no 'verified' or 'within_auth' flag. A gate that accepts such a field from a caller has surrendered authority to the caller. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED. No effectiveness claim attaches to this schema.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "request_id",
    "subject_id",
    "session_id",
    "contract_digest",
    "policy_digest",
    "effect_type",
    "target",
    "params_digest",
    "adapter_digest",
    "nonce",
    "sequence",
    "issued_at",
    "expires_at"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": {
      "const": "bssg.effect-request"
    },
    "spec_version": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/specVersion"
    },
    "request_id": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier",
      "description": "Unique identifier for this request. Uniqueness is asserted by the issuer and is not established by this schema."
    },
    "subject_id": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier",
      "description": "The acting system. Untrusted for all derived facts."
    },
    "session_id": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier"
    },
    "contract_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/digest",
      "description": "Digest over the canonical bytes (BSSG-CANON-1) of the contract body. The gate MUST recompute this digest rather than trust it."
    },
    "policy_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/digest"
    },
    "effect_type": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{1,63}$",
      "description": "A member of the contract's closed effect registry. Membership is checked by the gate, not by this schema."
    },
    "target": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["declared_uri", "canonical_uri", "target_class"],
      "properties": {
        "declared_uri": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 2048
        },
        "canonical_uri": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 2048,
          "description": "The subject's claim about the canonical form. The gate MUST recompute canonicalization and MUST treat a mismatch as FALSE and an uncanonicalizable target as UNKNOWN."
        },
        "target_class": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{1,63}$"
        }
      }
    },
    "params_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/digest",
      "description": "Digest over the canonical bytes of the effect parameters. Parameters themselves are transported out of band; the authorization binds only the digest."
    },
    "adapter_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/digest",
      "description": "Digest of the executor adapter that will perform the effect."
    },
    "nonce": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nonce"
    },
    "sequence": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "issued_at": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp"
    },
    "expires_at": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp"
    }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "specVersion": {
      "const": "0.1"
    },
    "identifier": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
    },
    "digest": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$",
      "description": "Lowercase hex SHA-256 over BSSG-CANON-1 canonical bytes. Hash agility is an OPEN DECISION (OD-4)."
    },
    "nonce": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{32}$"
    },
    "timestamp": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z$",
      "description": "RFC 3339 UTC with second precision and a literal Z. Any other lexical form MUST be treated as malformed, never coerced."
    }
  }
}


======== execution-receipt.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:execution-receipt",
  "title": "BSSG execution receipt (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "The record of a commit attempt. Commit is the point at which a one-time token is redeemed under compare-and-swap and all bindings and epochs are revalidated. A commit receipt records that the protocol obligation was discharged; it does not assert that the external target actually performed the effect. Unknown remote outcomes MUST be recorded as INDETERMINATE and MUST NOT be retried blindly. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "execution_id",
    "commit_token_id",
    "authorization_receipt_digest",
    "request_digest",
    "executed_target_canonical",
    "executed_params_digest",
    "adapter_digest",
    "outcome",
    "commit_time_epoch_seconds",
    "revalidation",
    "reason_codes",
    "signature"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": {
      "const": "bssg.execution-receipt"
    },
    "spec_version": {
      "const": "0.1"
    },
    "execution_id": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nullableDigest"
    },
    "commit_token_id": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 8,
          "maxLength": 128
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "authorization_receipt_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nullableDigest"
    },
    "request_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nullableDigest"
    },
    "executed_target_canonical": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "description": "MUST equal the canonical target bound in the authorization receipt. Any difference is a binding violation, not a widening to be tolerated."
    },
    "executed_params_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nullableDigest"
    },
    "adapter_digest": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/nullableDigest"
    },
    "outcome": {
      "enum": [
        "COMMITTED_PENDING_OUTCOME",
        "SUCCEEDED",
        "FAILED_NO_EFFECT",
        "INDETERMINATE",
        "QUARANTINED"
      ]
    },
    "commit_time_epoch_seconds": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "minimum": 0,
      "description": "Commit time in epoch seconds; null only on a fault path where the clock was unavailable (see reason E-CLOCK-UNAVAILABLE)."
    },
    "revalidation": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "result"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "result": {
          "enum": [
            "PASS",
            "FAIL",
            "UNKNOWN"
          ]
        },
        "authority_epoch": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch"
        },
        "evidence_epoch": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch"
        },
        "witness_epoch": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch"
        },
        "revalidated_at_epoch_seconds": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0
        }
      },
      "description": "Commit-time revalidation is a protocol obligation around the frozen five-conjunct kernel. It is not a sixth conjunct and MUST NOT be written into the frozen equation."
    },
    "reason_codes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^E-[A-Z0-9-]{2,64}$"
      }
    },
    "signature": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/signature"
    }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "epoch": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "nullableDigest": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "signature": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "alg",
        "kid",
        "envelope",
        "canonicalization"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "alg": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "kid": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "envelope": {
          "enum": [
            "detached-json",
            "COSE_Sign1",
            "COSE_Sign"
          ]
        },
        "canonicalization": {
          "const": "BSSG-CANON-1"
        },
        "signature_b64": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "cose_sign1_b64": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "verification_status": {
          "enum": [
            "verified_by_injected_verifier",
            "rejected_by_injected_verifier",
            "unknown",
            "not_performed_by_reference_gate"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}


======== lineage-record.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:lineage-record",
  "title": "BSSG lineage record (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "A hash-linked view of the records covering one session or one artifact. Lineage records claim continuity only for transformations that passed through a mediated adapter. Completeness outside the declared mediation boundary is not claimed and cannot be claimed. A lineage record with declared gaps MUST NOT report COMPLETE. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "lineage_id",
    "subject_id",
    "session_id",
    "nodes",
    "head_digest",
    "chain_alg",
    "completeness",
    "declared_gaps",
    "mediation_boundary_ref",
    "issued_at",
    "signature"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": { "const": "bssg.lineage-record" },
    "spec_version": { "const": "0.1" },
    "lineage_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "subject_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "session_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "nodes": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "required": ["node_id", "node_type", "digest", "parents"],
        "properties": {
          "node_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
          "node_type": {
            "enum": [
              "effect-request",
              "authorization-receipt",
              "witness-receipt",
              "execution-receipt",
              "correction-event",
              "decision-record"
            ]
          },
          "digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
          "parents": { "type": "array", "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" } },
          "superseded_by": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
          "recorded_at": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" }
        }
      }
    },
    "head_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
    "chain_alg": { "const": "sha256-parent-chain" },
    "completeness": {
      "enum": ["COMPLETE", "PARTIAL", "UNKNOWN"],
      "description": "COMPLETE means only: every mediated transformation inside the declared boundary has a recorded node. It never means that all causation has been reconstructed."
    },
    "declared_gaps": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "required": ["gap_kind", "description"],
        "properties": {
          "gap_kind": {
            "enum": [
              "unmediated-channel",
              "missing-witness-receipt",
              "uncorrected-descendant",
              "pre-boundary-artifact",
              "unknown"
            ]
          },
          "description": { "type": "string" },
          "related_digest": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" }
        }
      }
    },
    "mediation_boundary_ref": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "description": "Identifier of the deployment's declared effect-path inventory. Non-bypassability claims are scoped to exactly this inventory."
    },
    "correction_epoch": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
    "issued_at": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" },
    "signature": { "$ref": "#/$defs/signature" }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "identifier": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$" },
    "digest": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$" },
    "epoch": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 9007199254740991 },
    "timestamp": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z$"
    },
    "signature": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["alg", "kid", "envelope", "canonicalization"],
      "properties": {
        "alg": { "type": "string" },
        "kid": { "type": "string" },
        "envelope": { "enum": ["detached-json", "COSE_Sign1", "COSE_Sign"] },
        "canonicalization": { "const": "BSSG-CANON-1" },
        "signature_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "cose_sign1_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "verification_status": {
          "enum": [
            "verified_by_injected_verifier",
            "rejected_by_injected_verifier",
            "unknown",
            "not_performed_by_reference_gate"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}


======== witness-receipt.schema.schema.txt ========
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "urn:bssg:schema:0.1:witness-receipt",
  "title": "BSSG witness receipt (v0.1 DRAFT)",
  "description": "A signed statement by a witness about its own observed state and its coverage of a subject. A witness receipt attests observation and consistency with the witness's own prior state. It does NOT attest that the subject behaved correctly, that the witness is honest, or that the evidence is independent. WitnessHealthy MUST NOT be read as witness honesty. Status: DRAFT, NOT CARRIED.",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "record_type",
    "spec_version",
    "witness_id",
    "covered_subject",
    "covered_subject_domain",
    "independence_declaration",
    "within_auth_assessment",
    "epoch",
    "sequence",
    "checkpoint",
    "health",
    "observed_at",
    "expires_at",
    "signature"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "record_type": { "const": "bssg.witness-receipt" },
    "spec_version": { "const": "0.1" },
    "witness_id": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "covered_subject": { "$ref": "#/$defs/identifier" },
    "covered_subject_domain": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "description": "The administrative control domain of the covered subject, as declared by the witness."
    },
    "covered_effect_classes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": { "type": "string" }
    },
    "independence_declaration": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["control_domain", "subject_can_administer", "basis"],
      "properties": {
        "control_domain": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
        "subject_can_administer": {
          "enum": [true, false, "unknown"],
          "description": "Whether the subject or its transitive administrators can write, truncate, fork, sign for, reconfigure, or roll back this witness. Only an explicit false is admissible; true and 'unknown' MUST yield UNKNOWN at the gate. This is a DECLARATION and cannot be derived from a signature."
        },
        "basis": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "How the declaration was established: control-graph review identifier, operator attestation, or 'undetermined'."
        },
        "control_graph_ref": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },
    "within_auth_assessment": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["value", "method", "assessor"],
      "properties": {
        "value": {
          "enum": ["outside_subject_authority", "within_subject_authority", "unknown"],
          "description": "The WithinAuth control predicate of AX-SH 5, carried as an assessment. Evidence independence is an assumption plus a verified relation; it is NOT derivable from a valid signature."
        },
        "method": { "type": "string" },
        "assessor": { "type": "string" },
        "assessed_at": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" }
      }
    },
    "epoch": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
    "sequence": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
    "checkpoint": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["origin", "tree_size", "root_hash"],
      "properties": {
        "origin": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
        "tree_size": { "$ref": "#/$defs/epoch" },
        "root_hash": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" },
        "previous_root_hash": { "$ref": "#/$defs/digest" }
      },
      "description": "Checkpoint state permitting fork DETECTION by comparison across observers. Detection is not prevention."
    },
    "health": {
      "enum": ["HEALTHY", "DEGRADED", "FAILED", "UNKNOWN"],
      "description": "An availability and self-consistency signal. HEALTHY does not imply honesty, correctness, or non-collusion."
    },
    "health_reason": { "type": "string" },
    "observed_at": { "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp" },
    "expires_at": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/timestamp",
      "description": "End of the health lease. A gate MUST NOT reuse a lapsed lease."
    },
    "time_authority_id": { "type": "string" },
    "signature": { "$ref": "#/$defs/signature" }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "identifier": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$" },
    "digest": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$" },
    "epoch": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 9007199254740991 },
    "timestamp": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z$"
    },
    "signature": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": ["alg", "kid", "envelope", "canonicalization"],
      "properties": {
        "alg": { "type": "string" },
        "kid": { "type": "string" },
        "envelope": { "enum": ["detached-json", "COSE_Sign1", "COSE_Sign"] },
        "canonicalization": { "const": "BSSG-CANON-1" },
        "signature_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "cose_sign1_b64": { "type": "string" },
        "verification_status": {
          "enum": [
            "verified_by_injected_verifier",
            "rejected_by_injected_verifier",
            "unknown",
            "not_performed_by_reference_gate"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

---

# Bismillah ir-Rahman ir-Rahim
